P	本文件是 ACPOS-AI-01 的唯一系統邏輯母級設計輸出，目的是讓不同 AI、不同工程師、不同後續施工代理讀到同一份規格時，導出同一套核心邏輯。文件以「規範語義」而非敘事描述為主：凡涉及正式執行、資料採用、版本、權限、跨頁關係、學習、外部資料與副作用，均必須可由規則決定，不得靠個人解讀。

P	4. Information Lifecycle / 資訊生命週期
P	FIG-02｜資訊從來源到採用、再到 Experience/Learning 的完整生命週期。
P	正式生命週期不是「取得資料 → 丟給 AI」。任何資訊必須先有 Source Identity、Scope、Rights/Classification、Freshness、Exact Version 或 immutable snapshot；進入 AI 前形成 ContextSnapshot；AI/Tool 結果先成 Candidate；正式採用必須經 Domain/Governance owner；執行後的真實 Outcome 才能成 Experience。

P	9.2 T0–T7 Two-way Conflict Matrix
P	使用方式：先確認「既有 claim」與「新輸入 claim」指向相同 Canonical Claim Key（owner_scope + object_ref + property/claim_type + effective_version/time）。若不是同一 claim，兩者可並存；若是同一 claim，再套下表。
ROW	Code	Meaning
P	Code
P	Meaning
ROW	D	Exact value/provenance equivalent → DEDUPE / link same evidence.
P	D
P	Exact value/provenance equivalent → DEDUPE / link same evidence.
ROW	R	Same-tier divergent claim → create ConflictRecord；REVIEW_REQUIRED or owner-specific reconciliation.
P	R
P	Same-tier divergent claim → create ConflictRecord；REVIEW_REQUIRED or owner-specific reconciliation.

P	13. Context Budget Algorithm / Context 預算與壓縮演算法
P	Context Budget 必須是「不丟失治理語義」的資源分配，不是單純截字。Budget 可以包含 token、bytes、media count/size、retrieval calls、latency window；exact 數值由 implementation profile/Current Authority 綁定，本文件只定不可變的裁決順序。
ROW	Priority	Class	Content	Compression Rule
P	Priority
P	Class
P	Content
P	Compression Rule
ROW	P0	NON_DROPPABLE	Permission / classification / operation identity / exact version / required decision / required constraint / error-blocking conflict	不得刪、不得摘要到失去 exact identity。
P	P0
P	NON_DROPPABLE
P	Permission / classification / operation identity / exact version / required decision / required constraint / error-blocking conflict
P	不得刪、不得摘要到失去 exact identity。
ROW	P1	NON_DROPPABLE	Current object essential fields + required upstream/downstream exact refs	可結構化裁切非必要欄位，但 required field 不得缺。
P	P1
P	NON_DROPPABLE
P	Current object essential fields + required upstream/downstream exact refs
P	可結構化裁切非必要欄位，但 required field 不得缺。
ROW	P2	HIGH	Required approved knowledge / required evidence	可摘要內容，但保留 source/version/evidence refs。
P	P2
P	HIGH
P	Required approved knowledge / required evidence
P	可摘要內容，但保留 source/version/evidence refs。
ROW	P3	HIGH	Required validated experience / failure patterns	可聚合成統計/summary，保留 evidence sample refs。
P	P3
P	HIGH
P	Required validated experience / failure patterns
P	可聚合成統計/summary，保留 evidence sample refs。
ROW	P4	MEDIUM	Optional approved knowledge / optional relationships	先 dedupe，再 summary。
P	P4
P	MEDIUM
P	Optional approved knowledge / optional relationships
P	先 dedupe，再 summary。
ROW	P5	MEDIUM	Optional validated experience	優先保留與 domain/task/provider 高相似度。
P	P5
P	MEDIUM
P	Optional validated experience
P	優先保留與 domain/task/provider 高相似度。
ROW	P6	LOW	Conversation working context	保留最近且明確 decision refs；一般聊天可摘要。
P	P6
P	LOW
P	Conversation working context
P	保留最近且明確 decision refs；一般聊天可摘要。
ROW	P7	LOWEST	T6/T7 candidates / verbose raw text / redundant media	先移除或只留 pointer；正式需要時再 retrieve。
P	P7
P	LOWEST
P	T6/T7 candidates / verbose raw text / redundant media
P	先移除或只留 pointer；正式需要時再 retrieve。
P	13.1 Deterministic Budget Sequence
P	先計算 P0+P1 mandatory footprint，包含必要 media/attachment descriptors。
P	若 P0+P1 已超過 hard execution budget：BLOCK = CONTEXT_BUDGET_MANDATORY_OVERFLOW；不得靠刪除 constraint 取得 PASS。
P	對所有 refs 做 exact duplicate / semantic duplicate 檢查；同一 canonical source 只保留一次 payload，其他位置保留 reference。
P	依 P2→P7 順序加入；每層先 compact structure，再摘要 narrative，最後才 drop optional item。
P	摘要 MUST 保留 source_ref、exact_version/snapshot_hash、claim class、confidence/validation state、effective time。
P	Media 超限：先降 representation（thumbnail/metadata/derived feature），不得替換 exact asset/source identity。
P	Retrieval calls 超限：優先 required owner refs；optional external/semantic expansion 最先停止。
P	完成後重跑 Required-field validation + conflict scan + classification check。
P	Freeze ContextSnapshot；任何後續新增/刪除都必須建立新 snapshot/fingerprint。
P	14. Semantic Conflict Engine / 語義衝突引擎
ROW	Conflict Type	Trigger	Disposition	Resolution Rule
P	Conflict Type
P	Trigger
P	Disposition
P	Resolution Rule
ROW	SAME_VERSION_CONTENT_CONFLICT	同一 exact version identity 出現不同內容/hash	BLOCK	找 Current owner + provenance；不得自行選一份。
P	SAME_VERSION_CONTENT_CONFLICT
P	同一 exact version identity 出現不同內容/hash
P	BLOCK
P	找 Current owner + provenance；不得自行選一份。
ROW	DECISION_EXECUTION_DIVERGENCE	T1 決策目標與 T0 實際 outcome 不一致	REVIEW_REQUIRED / correction	保留兩者；不得 rewrite history。
P	DECISION_EXECUTION_DIVERGENCE
P	T1 決策目標與 T0 實際 outcome 不一致
P	REVIEW_REQUIRED / correction
P	保留兩者；不得 rewrite history。
ROW	KNOWLEDGE_FRESHNESS_CONFLICT	T4 新證據與 T2 Approved Knowledge 不一致	Candidate update	T2 保持 Current，建立 update review。
P	KNOWLEDGE_FRESHNESS_CONFLICT
P	T4 新證據與 T2 Approved Knowledge 不一致
P	Candidate update
P	T2 保持 Current，建立 update review。
ROW	CROSS_PROJECT_SCOPE_CONFLICT	Context candidate 來自不同 project/scope	BLOCK	只有明確授權 relation 才可跨 scope。
P	CROSS_PROJECT_SCOPE_CONFLICT
P	Context candidate 來自不同 project/scope
P	BLOCK
P	只有明確授權 relation 才可跨 scope。
ROW	STALE_CONTEXT_CONFLICT	Current source version > snapshot base	BLOCK effectful / branch read-only	re-resolve or explicit branch。
P	STALE_CONTEXT_CONFLICT
P	Current source version > snapshot base
P	BLOCK effectful / branch read-only
P	re-resolve or explicit branch。
ROW	PERMISSION_CONTEXT_CONFLICT	Snapshot 中包含 actor 不再可讀資料	BLOCK	re-resolve permission-filtered snapshot。
P	PERMISSION_CONTEXT_CONFLICT
P	Snapshot 中包含 actor 不再可讀資料
P	BLOCK
P	re-resolve permission-filtered snapshot。
ROW	CLASSIFICATION_PROVIDER_CONFLICT	Provider/tool policy 不允許 Context classification	BLOCK / eligible fallback	不得降級分類或偷偷刪敏感欄位後假裝相同 task。

P	18.1 Delivery Semantics
P	Canonical semantic = AT_LEAST_ONCE_DELIVERY + IDEMPOTENT_CONSUMER，除非 implementation authority 明確提供更強語義；不得假設 exactly-once。
P	Producer MUST emit event only after/with governed mutation persistence according to the selected transaction/outbox pattern; exact mechanism REQUIRE_EXACT_BINDING。
P	Consumer MUST dereference Current source owner for authoritative state when the operation requires Current truth；不得只相信 event copied payload。
P	Duplicate event_id/idempotency_key → ACK/return prior processing result，MUST_NOT duplicate effect。
P	Out-of-order event → compare aggregate_version/exact_version；stale event MUST_NOT roll back Current state。
P	Poison/non-retryable event → quarantine/dead-letter equivalent + audit + operator/review path；exact queue technology REQUIRE_EXACT_BINDING。

P	Scenario
P	Required Contract
P	Forbidden

P	Last-write-wins silent overwrite.

P	20. Idempotency Contract / 冪等契約
P	Idempotency 是副作用治理，不只是 HTTP 重送技巧。每個 effectful operation 必須明確定義「同一意圖」的 dedupe scope；exact storage/table/TTL 由 Current implementation authority 綁定。
ROW	Rule	Normative Definition
P	Rule
P	Normative Definition
ROW	Key Scope	idempotency_key MUST be interpreted together with canonical operation owner + actor/scope + target object + effect class；不得全站裸 key 共用而產生碰撞。
P	Key Scope
P	idempotency_key MUST be interpreted together with canonical operation owner + actor/scope + target object + effect class；不得全站裸 key 共用而產生碰撞。
ROW	Payload Digest	First accepted request stores canonical payload digest / semantic digest；algorithm REQUIRE_EXACT_BINDING。
P	Payload Digest
P	First accepted request stores canonical payload digest / semantic digest；algorithm REQUIRE_EXACT_BINDING。
ROW	Same Key + Same Payload	Return/reconcile original effect/result；MUST_NOT create second job/message/payment/publish/etc. effect。
P	Same Key + Same Payload
P	Return/reconcile original effect/result；MUST_NOT create second job/message/payment/publish/etc. effect。
ROW	Same Key + Different Payload	BLOCK = IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSE_CONFLICT；不得當作更新。
P	Same Key + Different Payload
P	BLOCK = IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSE_CONFLICT；不得當作更新。
ROW	In-flight Duplicate	Return IN_PROGRESS/accepted reference or attach to existing job according to runtime contract；不得平行重做 effect。
P	In-flight Duplicate
P	Return IN_PROGRESS/accepted reference or attach to existing job according to runtime contract；不得平行重做 effect。
ROW	Failed Before Side Effect	May retry with same key if failure class RETRYABLE and no external effect occurred.
P	Failed Before Side Effect
P	May retry with same key if failure class RETRYABLE and no external effect occurred.
ROW	Ambiguous External Effect	MUST reconcile external status by correlation/provider ref before retry.
P	Ambiguous External Effect
P	MUST reconcile external status by correlation/provider ref before retry.
ROW	Expiration	Exact TTL MUST be Current policy binding；不得早於業務副作用可重送風險窗。未綁定時 effectful implementation = REQUIRE_EXACT_BINDING/BLOCK。
P	Expiration
P	Exact TTL MUST be Current policy binding；不得早於業務副作用可重送風險窗。未綁定時 effectful implementation = REQUIRE_EXACT_BINDING/BLOCK。
ROW	Audit	All replays/duplicates/conflicts must record original effect ref and correlation chain.
P	Audit
P	All replays/duplicates/conflicts must record original effect ref and correlation chain.
P	21. Retry / Backoff / Fallback / Circuit Rules
ROW	Failure Class	Examples	Required Behavior	Forbidden
P	Failure Class
P	Examples
P	Required Behavior
P	Forbidden
ROW	RETRYABLE	Network reset、transient 5xx、provider temporary unavailable、selected timeout class、429 with policy	Bounded retry；exponential backoff + jitter or provider Retry-After；same idempotency context	No infinite retry
P	RETRYABLE
P	Network reset、transient 5xx、provider temporary unavailable、selected timeout class、429 with policy
P	Bounded retry；exponential backoff + jitter or provider Retry-After；same idempotency context
P	No infinite retry
ROW	NON_RETRYABLE	Schema validation、permission denied、rights/policy reject、unsupported capability、same-key/different-payload	Return/block immediately	No automatic retry
P	NON_RETRYABLE
P	Schema validation、permission denied、rights/policy reject、unsupported capability、same-key/different-payload
P	Return/block immediately
P	No automatic retry
ROW	REVIEW_REQUIRED	Unresolved truth conflict、ambiguous external effect after reconcile failure、high-risk adoption、owner ambiguity	Create review item and stop dependent effect	No auto approval
P	REVIEW_REQUIRED
P	Unresolved truth conflict、ambiguous external effect after reconcile failure、high-risk adoption、owner ambiguity
P	Create review item and stop dependent effect
P	No auto approval
ROW	FALLBACK_ALLOWED	Primary eligible provider/tool unavailable but alternate passes same hard capability/rights/classification/policy gates	Recompile from canonical meaning for alternate adapter	Never reuse provider-specific compiled payload blindly
P	FALLBACK_ALLOWED
P	Primary eligible provider/tool unavailable but alternate passes same hard capability/rights/classification/policy gates
P	Recompile from canonical meaning for alternate adapter
P	Never reuse provider-specific compiled payload blindly
ROW	BLOCK	Exact version missing、classification unresolved、Current owner missing、mandatory context overflow、hard gate fails with no equivalent path	Persist block evidence	No degradation that changes meaning
P	BLOCK
P	Exact version missing、classification unresolved、Current owner missing、mandatory context overflow、hard gate fails with no equivalent path
P	Persist block evidence
P	No degradation that changes meaning

P	State
P	Entry
P	Behavior
P	Exit

P	OPEN
P	Health/policy breaker triggered

P	24.1 Delete Decision Sequence
P	Resolve canonical owner and artifact classification.
P	Check forward references.
P	Check reverse references / dependency graph.
P	Check active Runtime/Workflow reachability.
P	Check audit/regression/historical-retention role.
P	Check privacy/rights/legal hold.
P	If any REQUIRED reference remains → BLOCK hard delete；archive/tombstone may be allowed by owner policy.
P	Persist deletion disposition + evidence + successor/tombstone reference when applicable.
P	Revalidate search/index/projection consumers so deleted artifact cannot appear as Current.

P	33. Construction Handoff / 後續施工契約
P	ACPOS-AI-01 的 Design Output 只交付「必須存在的邏輯與驗收條件」。進入實作時必須 Search-before-create，優先找到 Current Owner / UID / Registry / Implementation / Test / Evidence；若 owner 存在，修改原 owner；只有完整證明不存在 Governed Unit 且 Mother 允許時才可建立新的 owner。

ROW	Design Closure Boundary 這三份補齊文件只完成設計層閉環；GitHub 程式碼、Runtime、E2E 與 Production 狀態仍必須由後續 fresh execution evidence 個別證明。

P	G. Construction Handoff Rule
P	施工 AI MUST resolve each UI control through this exact order: Page UID → Section/Region → Control UID → Trigger/Operation or Action UID → Gate → Permission → Input/Payload → Method/Path or shared Runtime Operation → Persistence/Provider Owner → Audit/Event → Success/Failure/Recovery → Next Page/Handoff. UI_ONLY/READ_ONLY controls MUST NOT create a write API. System-only operations MUST NOT cause a new visible button unless this closure/source explicitly materializes that control.
P	When a provider/platform/environment value is runtime-specific, the exact binding is the registered Profile/Policy/Environment key named in this annex. Missing required value is a deterministic BLOCK; it is not permission to guess.
P	Unified Page / Control / Action / API / DB / Runtime Construction Matrix
P	[ACPOS-20260921-UNIFIED-CONTROL-RUNTIME-MATRIX-V3] This matrix is the direct construction lookup. Existing source identifiers are preserved. New Action/Gate/Control identities are created only when exhaustive uploaded-source scan found the semantic control/operation but no exact UID/path. Existing exact Method/Path always wins; otherwise effectful operations use POST /v1/commands/{actionUid} through the Shared Typed Command Runtime. UI/read-only controls explicitly bind to N/A or projection runtime and MUST NOT create writes.

If API Basis is NEW_QUERY_AUTHORITY_AFTER_GLOBAL_SOURCE_EXHAUSTION, use only the Shared Typed Query Runtime GET endpoint shown.
